vibecomfy: warm-runtime sessions (keep models resident across generations by reusing Comfy's existing model_management, not reinventing it).

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy
Embedded ComfyUI fork in use: hiddenswitch (path: /Users/user_c042661f/.cache/uv/git-v0/checkouts/b790d0c34622492a/c5ed9402/)

# Problem
Today both vibecomfy runtime paths are cold-per-call:
- Embedded: vibecomfy/runtime/run.py:125-138 enters/exits a fresh Comfy() context per run. The exit calls embedded_comfy_client.py:_cleanup -> model_management.unload_all_models() + gc + soft_empty_cache. Models are dumped after every generation.
- Managed server: vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:16 starts comfyui as a subprocess, queues once, terminates. Same outcome at the process level.

ComfyUI itself already keeps models warm by default — current_loaded_models persists in process, load_models_gpu reuses resident copies, eviction is pressure-driven, OOM is auto-handled (execution.py:735-737), and DISABLE_SMART_MEMORY is the only thing that forces unload after each prompt. So the work is structural: stop tearing the runtime down.

# Goal
Add session-aware warm runtime to vibecomfy with two backends sharing one abstraction:
1. Long-lived managed Comfy server (HTTP control plane) — default for CLI users.
2. Long-lived embedded Comfy() context — for in-process callers (runpod worker, tests, tight matrix loops).

Default policy: keep warm. Explicit flush available. Auto-flush on detected model-set change under VRAM pressure.

# Verified facts (don't re-research)
- POST /free and POST /api/free both exist in the embedded hiddenswitch fork at comfy/cmd/server.py:1041 with /api mirror auto-added at :1357. Payload: {"unload_models": bool, "free_memory": bool}. They set queue flags via prompt_queue.set_flag(...) — async, take effect at next prompt boundary, not synchronously.
- CLI flags exist in comfy/cli_args.py:115-160: --highvram, --lowvram, --normalvram, --reserve-vram <GB>, --disable-smart-memory, --cache-classic, --cache-lru N, --cache-none.
- Comfy.clear_cache() (embedded_comfy_client.py:385) and Comfy.reconfigure() (:398) already exposed; reconfigure uses MODEL_MANAGEMENT_ARGS fingerprint to avoid unnecessary subprocess cycling.
- The cleanup that nukes warm state lives in embedded_comfy_client.py:_cleanup (lines ~149-169) and is called from Comfy.__aexit__ (line ~481).

# Design constraints / refinements over the user's notes
- Do NOT default to --highvram (breaks multi-model workflows that need offload). Default to Comfy's smart memory.
- /free is asynchronous — flush API must either drain via a no-op prompt or document the queue-flag semantics. Tests must account for this.
- Auto-flush should only trigger on model-set fingerprint change AND VRAM-headroom-below-threshold. Input/seed/prompt changes must NOT trigger flush.
- VIBECOMFY_WARM=auto|always|never controls policy (auto default).
- Session lifecycle: daemon-style for CLI (pidfile + URL file under out/sessions/<id>/) so multiple `vibecomfy run` invocations share one warm pool. Embedded session is just a Python context manager.
- Browser-only tasks: there are none in this plan (no UI work).
- No hard-halt prereq clauses: each milestone is independently shippable.

# Out of scope for this megaplan
- Multi-GPU session pooling.
- Distributed/remote session sharing across machines.
- Workflow-graph-level caching (Comfy already has --cache-lru / --cache-classic).
- Replacing the existing one-shot run/run_embedded APIs — they should remain as thin wrappers around the new session.
- Cloud/RunPod orchestration changes (the runpod worker can adopt EmbeddedSession in a separate plan).

# Suggested milestone shape (megaplan can refine during prep/plan)
M1: Session abstraction + embedded backend
  - vibecomfy/runtime/session.py: VibeSession protocol + EmbeddedSession holding a long-lived Comfy() across multiple .run(workflow) calls.
  - .flush() -> Comfy.clear_cache(); .reconfigure(config) -> Comfy.reconfigure().
  - Refactor run_embedded() to be a one-shot wrapper around EmbeddedSession.
  - Tests: two sequential runs in one session reuse models (assert via inspecting comfy.model_management.current_loaded_models after each run, or via a stub model manager); flush() drops them; reconfigure with same fingerprint is a no-op.

M2: Managed server backend
  - ServerSession: keeps comfy_server subprocess alive across .run() calls. Start = spawn + wait-ready. .flush() = POST /api/free with appropriate flags. .stop() = SIGTERM with timeout fallback to SIGKILL.
  - Daemon mode: `vibecomfy session start [--id NAME] [--vram-policy ...]` writes pidfile + URL to out/sessions/<id>/; `vibecomfy session stop <id>`; `vibecomfy session list`; `vibecomfy session flush <id>`.
  - `vibecomfy run` auto-discovers active session if one exists, else falls back to one-shot managed mode.
  - Tests: session start/stop, flush via HTTP, two runs share warmth, daemon survives across CLI invocations.

M3: Auto-flush policy + fingerprinting
  - vibecomfy/runtime/model_fingerprint.py: extract loader-node identity tuples from a workflow (CheckpointLoaderSimple.ckpt_name, LoraLoader.lora_name, UNETLoader.unet_name, VAELoader.vae_name, CLIPLoader.clip_name, DualCLIPLoader, etc. — discover the canonical set from the embedded fork's nodes).
  - Session.run() compares current workflow fingerprint to last-run fingerprint. If different AND free VRAM < threshold (configurable, default e.g. 2GB), call flush() before queueing.
  - VIBECOMFY_WARM env var: auto (default) | always | never.
  - Tests: same model + different seed -> no flush; different ckpt -> flush; auto-flush gated by VRAM headroom (mock get_free_memory).

M4: VRAM/cache flag plumbing + docs
  - Session config accepts: vram_policy ("auto"|"high"|"low"|"normal"), reserve_vram_gb, cache_policy ("smart"|"classic"|"lru:N"|"none"), disable_smart_memory.
  - Threaded through to embedded Configuration AND server CLI args.
  - docs/runtime_lifecycle.md: add "Warm sessions" section. docs/runtime_surface.md: document VibeSession API.
  - Smoke test: `vibecomfy session start && vibecomfy run x.json && vibecomfy run y.json && vibecomfy session stop` — second run is materially faster than first (timing-based, with tolerance).

# Open questions for the planning step
- Should `vibecomfy run` lazily start a session on first call and reuse it (implicit warm) vs always require `session start`? Lean toward explicit start for predictability.
- Does the runpod worker (reigh-workspace/reigh-worker) want to consume EmbeddedSession directly or talk to a local managed session? Probably embedded — it owns the process lifecycle already. Out of scope for this plan, but the API should not preclude it.
- Do we need partial flush (just CLIP, just VAE, just UNet)? Probably no — Comfy's pressure-driven eviction handles per-model decisions; only expose all-or-nothing flush at the session boundary.

# Success criteria (suggested — planning phase should refine)
- A `VibeSession` abstraction exists with both Embedded and Server backends.
- Two sequential `.run()` calls in one session share warm models (verified by test, not just timing).
- `flush()` is explicit and works for both backends. For the server backend, the test accounts for /free's queue-flag semantics.
- `vibecomfy session start/stop/list/flush` CLI commands exist and survive across separate `vibecomfy` invocations.
- Auto-flush policy fires on model-set change under VRAM pressure, never on seed/input changes.
- Existing `run` and `run_embedded` one-shot APIs continue to work unchanged (backwards compatibility).
- Documentation updated in docs/runtime_lifecycle.md and docs/runtime_surface.md.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Warm-runtime VibeSession (embedded + managed backends)

## Overview

Both runtime entry points are cold-per-call today: `run_embedded()` enters/exits a fresh `Comfy()` per generation (`vibecomfy/runtime/run.py:125-138`) whose `__aexit__` runs `unload_all_models()`, and `run()` spawns/terminates `comfyui serve` per call (`vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:27-50`). ComfyUI itself keeps models warm in-process and evicts under pressure — the work here is structural.

We add a `VibeSession` abstraction with two backends — `EmbeddedSession` (one long-lived `Comfy()`) and `ServerSession` (one long-lived `comfyui serve` subprocess) — plus a CLI session pool under `out/sessions/<id>/` (pidfile + URL file) so multiple `vibecomfy run` invocations share one warm pool. Existing `run`/`run_embedded` stay as one-shot wrappers (backwards-compatible).

Per the brief, the long-lived **managed** server is the default for CLI users. The plan changes `vibecomfy run`'s default runtime to `auto` and routes through a discovered session when one exists. The schema provider is taught to share the same URL so we don't accidentally spin up a second cold subprocess for `/object_info`. Default policy: keep-warm with smart memory; explicit `flush()` and a fingerprint-gated auto-flush handle model swaps under VRAM pressure.

Key constraints kept from the brief:
- `/free` is async (queue flag) — `ServerSession.flush()` documents this; tests assert payload + endpoint, not synchronous unload.
- Don't default to `--highvram`.
- Auto-flush gated on **fingerprint change AND** free-VRAM-below-threshold; seed/prompt/input changes never trigger flush.
- Embedded fork not installed locally — tests use the existing `sys.modules` stub pattern from `tests/test_runtime_run.py:140-149`.

Settled decisions carried forward:
- `vibecomfy run --runtime auto` is the new default; auto-attaches to a warm session if any.
- Schema provider URL discovery happens before `get_schema_provider` construction; same URL threaded into both call sites.
- Daemon folded into `vibecomfy/commands/session.py` (single file), invoked as `python -m vibecomfy.commands.session --daemon --id <NAME> --config <JSON>`.
- Managed-mode warmth verified at the test layer (subprocess spawn count == 1 + `/api/free` payload assertions); operator smoke uses embedded backend.
- ServerSession default port 8188 with `--port` override; auto-flush threshold default 2.0 GB.
- Pattern-based loader fingerprint: any `class_type` containing `"Loader"` contributes its non-edge string-valued slots; empty `OVERRIDES_INCLUDE`/`OVERRIDES_EXCLUDE` reserved for exceptions.
- Shared runtime helpers move from `run.py` into `session.py` (one-direction import).
- `tests/test_cli.py:25-40` registry assertion gets `"session"` inserted between `"runtime"` and `"logs"`.

Resolution added in this revision:
- **FLAG-009 / correctness (embedded-config carry-through)**: `_embedded_configuration` (`vibecomfy/runtime/run.py:163-181`) currently merges arbitrary keys from `workflow.metadata["comfy_configuration"]` into `default_configuration()`. Repo ready-templates rely on raw HiddenSwitch keys outside the typed `SessionConfig` shape — verified at `ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_t2v.py:185` and `ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame.py:347`, both setting `{"reserve_vram": 12, "cache_none": True, "fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc": True}`. To preserve this contract: `SessionConfig` gains `extra: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)`. `SessionConfig.from_workflow_metadata` partitions known keys into typed fields and routes everything else into `extra`. `_embedded_configuration` applies typed fields first, then merges `extra`, then applies the `VIBECOMFY_COMFY_CONFIGURATION` env override last — preserving the existing override precedence at `run.py:163-181`.

Out of scope: multi-GPU pooling, distributed sessions, workflow-graph caching, replacing one-shot APIs, RunPod orchestration changes.

## Phase 1: Embedded session foundation

### Step 1: Add VibeSession protocol + EmbeddedSession + relocate shared helpers (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`, `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`. To avoid circular imports between `session.py` and `run.py`, this module **owns** the shared runtime helpers: move `RunResult` (`runtime/run.py:19-25`), `_prepare_prompt` (`runtime/run.py:28-42`), `_run_metadata` (`runtime/run.py:76-96`), `_collect_output_paths` (`runtime/run.py:204-215`), `_git_sha` (`runtime/run.py:99-109`), and `_embedded_configuration` (`runtime/run.py:163-181`) from `run.py` into `session.py`. `run.py` imports them from `session.py`; `session.py` does **not** import from `run.py`.
2. **Update** `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py:1-11` so `RunResult` continues to be importable as `from vibecomfy.runtime import RunResult` (re-export through `run.py` or directly from `session.py`).
3. **Add** `SessionConfig` dataclass:
   ```python
   @dataclass
   class SessionConfig:
       vram_policy: Literal["auto","high","low","normal"] = "auto"
       reserve_vram_gb: float | None = None
       cache_policy: Literal["smart","classic","none"] | str = "smart"  # also "lru:N"
       disable_smart_memory: bool = False
       warm_policy: Literal["auto","always","never"] = "auto"
       auto_flush_vram_threshold_gb: float = 2.0
       port: int = 8188  # server-only
       extra: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)  # raw HiddenSwitch keys not yet promoted
   ```
   Class method `SessionConfig.from_workflow_metadata(workflow) -> SessionConfig` reads `workflow.metadata.get("comfy_configuration", {})` and partitions:
   - Keys that map onto typed fields (e.g. `reserve_vram` → `reserve_vram_gb`, `cache_none` → `cache_policy="none"`, `cache_classic` → `cache_policy="classic"`, `disable_smart_memory` → `disable_smart_memory`) are converted and stored typed.
   - All remaining keys (e.g. `fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc` from `ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_t2v.py:185`) are stored verbatim in `extra`.
   Document in the `SessionConfig` docstring: `extra` is intentionally an escape hatch for raw HiddenSwitch keys not yet promoted to typed fields; over time, frequently-used keys should be promoted.
4. **Add** `class VibeSession(Protocol)`: `async start()`, `async run(workflow, *, backend="api") -> RunResult`, `async flush(*, unload_models=True, free_memory=True)`, `async reconfigure(config)`, `async stop()`, attribute `last_fingerprint`.
5. **Add** `class EmbeddedSession(VibeSession)`: holds one long-lived `Comfy()` across calls. `start()` instantiates and `__aenter__`s once. `run()` calls `comfy.queue_prompt_api(api_dict)`. `flush()` → `comfy.clear_cache()`. `reconfigure(SessionConfig)` → `comfy.reconfigure(...)`. `stop()` calls `__aexit__(None, None, None)` and tolerates the `model_mmap_residency` AttributeError currently swallowed at `run.py:135-138`.
6. **Extend** the relocated `_embedded_configuration(config: SessionConfig) -> Configuration` in `session.py` to apply, in order: (a) typed `SessionConfig` fields → corresponding HiddenSwitch keys (vram_policy → highvram/lowvram/normalvram booleans; reserve_vram_gb → reserve_vram; cache_policy → cache_classic/cache_lru/cache_none; disable_smart_memory); (b) `config.extra` keys merged on top (so `fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc=True` from ready templates lands on the Configuration object); (c) `VIBECOMFY_COMFY_CONFIGURATION` env override merged last. This preserves the existing precedence at `runtime/run.py:163-181`.
7. **Add pattern-based** `model_fingerprint(api_dict) -> tuple` in `session.py`. Algorithm:
   - For each `(node_id, node)` in `api_dict.items()`:
     - `is_loader = (("Loader" in node["class_type"]) or (node["class_type"] in OVERRIDES_INCLUDE)) and node["class_type"] not in OVERRIDES_EXCLUDE`.
     - If `is_loader`: for each `(slot, value)` in `node["inputs"].items()`:
       - Skip edge references (list of `[node_id, output_idx]`).
       - Skip non-strings and empty strings.
       - Emit `(class_type, slot, value)`.
   - Return `tuple(sorted(triples))`.
   - `OVERRIDES_INCLUDE: frozenset[str] = frozenset()`; `OVERRIDES_EXCLUDE: frozenset[str] = frozenset()` — explicitly noted as the soft-coverage gap for non-`Loader`-suffix model nodes.

### Step 2: Auto-flush gate inside `EmbeddedSession.run` (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Compute** `fp = model_fingerprint(api_dict)` before queueing. Resolve effective policy: `os.environ.get("VIBECOMFY_WARM")` overrides `config.warm_policy` if set.
   - `never` → flush then queue. `always` → never auto-flush. `auto` → flush iff `last_fingerprint is not None and fp != last_fingerprint and _free_vram_gb() < threshold`.
2. After successful queue: `self.last_fingerprint = fp`.
3. **Add** `_free_vram_gb()` lazy-importing `comfy.model_management.get_free_memory()`; returns `float("inf")` on import/AttributeError.

### Step 3: Refactor `run_embedded` into a one-shot wrapper (`vibecomfy/runtime/run.py`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Rewrite** `run_embedded()` (`runtime/run.py:116-156`) as:
   ```python
   from .session import EmbeddedSession, SessionConfig
   ...
   session = EmbeddedSession(SessionConfig.from_workflow_metadata(workflow))
   await session.start()
   try:
       return await session.run(workflow, backend=backend)
   finally:
       await session.stop()
   ```
2. **Delete** the relocated helpers from `run.py` and import them from `session.py`. The `run()`/`smoke_runtime()` one-shot managed-server flow stays in `run.py`.

### Step 4: Tests for the embedded session (`tests/test_runtime_session.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `tests/test_runtime_session.py` reusing the `sys.modules` injection pattern from `tests/test_runtime_run.py:140-149`. `FakeComfy` records enter/exit counts plus `queue_prompt_api`/`clear_cache`/`reconfigure` calls.
2. **Cover (must)**:
   - Two `session.run(workflow_a)` calls produce **one** `__aenter__`/`__aexit__` pair.
   - `flush()` calls `clear_cache()` exactly once.
   - `reconfigure(SessionConfig(...))` passes a Configuration whose typed fields reflect SessionConfig.
   - **`SessionConfig.from_workflow_metadata` carry-through (FLAG-009)**: build a `VibeWorkflow` whose `metadata["comfy_configuration"] = {"reserve_vram": 12, "cache_none": True, "fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc": True}` (matching `ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame.py:347`). Assert: `config.reserve_vram_gb == 12`, `config.cache_policy == "none"`, `config.extra == {"fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc": True}`. Then call `_embedded_configuration(config)` and assert the resulting Configuration object has `reserve_vram == 12`, `cache_none == True`, AND `fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc == True`.
   - **Pattern-based fingerprint correctness** — three snapshot tests:
     - Load `tests/snapshots/wan_t2v.api.json`. Same dict twice → same fingerprint. Mutate `widget_0` of `UNETLoader` → different fingerprint.
     - Load `tests/snapshots/ltx2_3_t2v.api.json`. Assert the fingerprint contains tuples for `LowVRAMCheckpointLoader` (line 94), `LowVRAMAudioVAELoader` (line 125), `LTXAVTextEncoderLoader` (line 383), `LoraLoaderModelOnly` (line 359). Mutate `LowVRAMCheckpointLoader.ckpt_name` → fingerprint changes; mutate an unrelated KSampler `widget_0` integer → fingerprint unchanged.
     - Synthetic dict with `class_type="DualCLIPLoaderGGUF"` and `class_type="UnetLoaderGGUF"` (matches `ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame.py:172-186`) — assert values are picked up. Edge references (`[node_id, idx]` lists) on the same loader are NOT included.
   - Auto-flush truth table with mocked `_free_vram_gb`: same fingerprint + low VRAM → no flush; different fingerprint + low VRAM (0.5 GB) → flush; different fingerprint + high VRAM (10 GB) → no flush; same loaders + different KSampler seed → no flush.
   - `VIBECOMFY_WARM=never` flushes before every run; `VIBECOMFY_WARM=always` never auto-flushes.
3. **Confirm** existing `tests/test_runtime_run.py` still passes — including `test_embedded_configuration_uses_hiddenswitch_configuration_object` (which exercises the env-override path) which now calls through `session._embedded_configuration` via `run.py`'s re-export.

## Phase 2: Managed server session + warm-default CLI

### Step 5: ServerSession backend (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`, `vibecomfy/runtime/client.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Extract** the subprocess startup from `runtime/server.py:27-41` into a private helper `_spawn_comfy_server(config) -> tuple[Process, str, log_handle]` in `session.py`. Translates `SessionConfig` to CLI flags: `--highvram|--lowvram|--normalvram` (skip when `auto`), `--reserve-vram <GB>`, `--disable-smart-memory`, `--cache-classic|--cache-lru N|--cache-none` (skip when `smart`), `--port <P>`. Reuse the existing 120-iteration `/system_stats` readiness loop.
2. **Add** `ServerSession(VibeSession)` to `session.py`. `start()` calls `_spawn_comfy_server`. `run()` calls `ComfyClient(self.url).queue_prompt(api_dict)` with the same auto-flush gate. `flush()` POSTs `/api/free` with `{"unload_models": True, "free_memory": True}`. `reconfigure(config)` is restart-on-change. `stop()` SIGTERMs/15s/SIGKILL per `server.py:44-50`.
3. **Add** `ComfyClient.free(unload_models, free_memory)` in `runtime/client.py:30+`. One-line docstring: queue-async semantics.

### Step 6: Daemon CLI + warm-default routing + registry test update (`vibecomfy/commands/session.py`, `vibecomfy/commands/run.py`, `vibecomfy/commands/__init__.py`, `tests/test_cli.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `vibecomfy/commands/session.py` containing argparse wiring for `start/stop/list/flush/status` AND the in-process daemon entrypoint. Single file. Invokable as `python -m vibecomfy.commands.session --daemon --id NAME --config <json>`; when invoked that way, instantiates `ServerSession(SessionConfig.from_dict(...))`, calls `start()`, writes `out/sessions/<id>/{pid,url,config.json}`, installs SIGTERM handler that calls `await session.stop()` and removes the pidfile, then sleeps on an `asyncio.Event`.
2. **Daemonize** from `session start` by `subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-m", "vibecomfy.commands.session", "--daemon", "--id", id_, "--config", json.dumps(config)], stdout=DEVNULL, stderr=open(log_path,"ab"), start_new_session=True)`. Parent waits up to 120 s for `out/sessions/<id>/url`.
3. **Subcommands**: `start [--id NAME=default] [--vram-policy auto|high|low|normal] [--reserve-vram-gb F] [--cache-policy smart|classic|lru:N|none] [--disable-smart-memory] [--warm-policy auto|always|never] [--port P=8188]`, `stop <id>`, `list`, `flush <id> [--unload-models/--no-unload-models] [--free-memory/--no-free-memory]`, `status <id>`.
4. **Add** `runtime/session.py::find_active_session(id="default") -> str | None`: read `out/sessions/<id>/url`; if pidfile present and `os.kill(pid, 0)` succeeds, return URL; else clean up and return `None`.
5. **Register** in `vibecomfy/commands/__init__.py:14-28` by adding `CommandSpec("session", "vibecomfy.commands.session")` between `"runtime"` and `"logs"`.
6. **Update** `tests/test_cli.py:25-40` to insert `"session"` between `"runtime"` and `"logs"` in the asserted ordered list. Required so `test_cli_command_registry_is_explicit_and_ordered` and `test_build_parser_registers_all_known_commands` keep passing.
7. **Make warm-managed the default for `vibecomfy run`** (`vibecomfy/commands/run.py`):
   - Change `--runtime` default from `"embedded"` to `"auto"`.
   - At the top of `_cmd_run`, **before** constructing the schema provider: discover the session URL via `find_active_session("default")` (only when `args.runtime in ("auto","server")` and `--server-url` not set). Resolve effective runtime: `auto` + warm session → server against that URL; `auto` + no warm → embedded one-shot; `server` + warm → reuse it; `server` + no warm → existing one-shot managed.
   - Pass the URL into `get_schema_provider("auto", server_url=session_url)` and into `run_sync(..., server_url=session_url, ...)`.

### Step 7: Tests for ServerSession + CLI (`tests/test_runtime_session.py`, `tests/test_session_cli.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **In** `tests/test_runtime_session.py` add `ServerSession` tests with monkeypatched `asyncio.create_subprocess_exec` and stub `httpx.AsyncClient`. Cover (must):
   - `start()` invokes subprocess with expected CLI argv given `SessionConfig(vram_policy="high", reserve_vram_gb=2.0, cache_policy="lru:3", disable_smart_memory=True, port=8200)`.
   - Two `session.run(...)` calls share **one** subprocess (assert spawn count == 1).
   - `flush()` POSTs `/api/free` with `{"unload_models": True, "free_memory": True}`.
   - `reconfigure(same_config)` is a no-op; `reconfigure(different_cli_args)` triggers stop+start.
   - `stop()` SIGTERMs once, falls back to SIGKILL after 15 s.
2. **Create** `tests/test_session_cli.py`. Mock `subprocess.Popen` so the "daemon" is replaced by a synchronous helper that writes `out/sessions/<id>/{pid,url,config.json}` with a synthetic alive pid. Assert: start returns 0 + creates files; list prints active id+URL; flush calls `ComfyClient.free(True, True)`; stop removes files; `find_active_session` returns URL when pid alive, returns `None` and cleans up when pid is dead (use `2**31 - 1`).
3. **Add** `tests/test_runtime_run.py` regression: `_cmd_run` with `--runtime auto` and a fake active session in `out/sessions/default/url` calls `run_sync` with that URL **and** constructs the schema provider with the same URL.

## Phase 3: Documentation + smoke

### Step 8: Docs (`docs/runtime_lifecycle.md`, `docs/runtime_surface.md`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Append** "Warm sessions" section to `docs/runtime_lifecycle.md`: explain `EmbeddedSession` vs `ServerSession`, the `vibecomfy session` CLI, the warm-policy rules, `/api/free` queue-flag semantics, `VIBECOMFY_WARM`, the pattern-based loader fingerprint, the `SessionConfig.extra` escape hatch (and that workflows with raw `comfy_configuration` keys like `fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc` continue to work), and the new `auto` default routing.
2. **Append** a `VibeSession` API block to `docs/runtime_surface.md` under "Embedded Surface" (start/run/flush/reconfigure/stop + SessionConfig fields including `extra`).
3. **Update** `docs/runtime_lifecycle.md:3` to reflect `vibecomfy run` now defaults to `auto`.

### Step 9: Smoke (`scripts/warm_session_smoke.py`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Add** a manual smoke script using the **embedded** session — runs two cheap workflows back-to-back through one `EmbeddedSession`, prints elapsed deltas. Server-mode timing measures only queue latency; this script avoids that pitfall.

## Execution Order

1. Steps 1–4: embedded session + helper relocation + extra-config carry-through + tests first.
2. Steps 5–7: ServerSession + daemon CLI + warm-default routing + `test_cli.py` registry update.
3. Step 8: docs.
4. Step 9: smoke script.
Each phase is independently shippable.

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_cli.py` — registry assertion still passes after adding `"session"`.
2. `pytest tests/test_runtime_run.py` — `run_embedded` refactor + helper relocation preserves existing behavior; `test_embedded_configuration_uses_hiddenswitch_configuration_object` continues to pass via the relocated helper.
3. `pytest tests/test_runtime_session.py` — embedded + server session unit tests, including `from_workflow_metadata` carry-through test and pattern-based fingerprint snapshot tests against `wan_t2v.api.json` and `ltx2_3_t2v.api.json`.
4. `pytest tests/test_session_cli.py` — daemon CLI + `find_active_session` flow.
5. Full `pytest` — confirm no collateral damage.
6. Manual on RunPod (info-only): `python scripts/warm_session_smoke.py` — second `.run()` materially faster; `comfy.model_management.current_loaded_models` non-empty between calls.

    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Embedded configuration compatibility: Checked current ready templates and found many set `workflow.metadata['comfy_configuration']` to raw HiddenSwitch keys such as `reserve_vram`, `cache_none`, and `fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc` (for example `ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_t2v.py:185` and `ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame.py:347`). The v3 plan says `SessionConfig.from_workflow_metadata()` reads this metadata, but the declared `SessionConfig` fields do not include arbitrary raw configuration keys like `fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc`, and Step 3 passes only `SessionConfig.from_workflow_metadata(workflow)` into `EmbeddedSession`; unless raw extra config is carried through explicitly, the existing embedded configuration override path can lose behavior.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  }
]

    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

      ]

    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-146",

  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 6,
    "plan_count": 3,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-143",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },

For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.

        Idea:
vibecomfy: warm-runtime sessions (keep models resident across generations by reusing Comfy's existing model_management, not reinventing it).

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy
Embedded ComfyUI fork in use: hiddenswitch (path: /Users/user_c042661f/.cache/uv/git-v0/checkouts/b790d0c34622492a/c5ed9402/)

# Problem
Today both vibecomfy runtime paths are cold-per-call:
- Embedded: vibecomfy/runtime/run.py:125-138 enters/exits a fresh Comfy() context per run. The exit calls embedded_comfy_client.py:_cleanup -> model_management.unload_all_models() + gc + soft_empty_cache. Models are dumped after every generation.
- Managed server: vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:16 starts comfyui as a subprocess, queues once, terminates. Same outcome at the process level.

ComfyUI itself already keeps models warm by default — current_loaded_models persists in process, load_models_gpu reuses resident copies, eviction is pressure-driven, OOM is auto-handled (execution.py:735-737), and DISABLE_SMART_MEMORY is the only thing that forces unload after each prompt. So the work is structural: stop tearing the runtime down.

# Goal
Add session-aware warm runtime to vibecomfy with two backends sharing one abstraction:
1. Long-lived managed Comfy server (HTTP control plane) — default for CLI users.
2. Long-lived embedded Comfy() context — for in-process callers (runpod worker, tests, tight matrix loops).

Default policy: keep warm. Explicit flush available. Auto-flush on detected model-set change under VRAM pressure.

# Verified facts (don't re-research)
- POST /free and POST /api/free both exist in the embedded hiddenswitch fork at comfy/cmd/server.py:1041 with /api mirror auto-added at :1357. Payload: {"unload_models": bool, "free_memory": bool}. They set queue flags via prompt_queue.set_flag(...) — async, take effect at next prompt boundary, not synchronously.
- CLI flags exist in comfy/cli_args.py:115-160: --highvram, --lowvram, --normalvram, --reserve-vram <GB>, --disable-smart-memory, --cache-classic, --cache-lru N, --cache-none.
- Comfy.clear_cache() (embedded_comfy_client.py:385) and Comfy.reconfigure() (:398) already exposed; reconfigure uses MODEL_MANAGEMENT_ARGS fingerprint to avoid unnecessary subprocess cycling.
- The cleanup that nukes warm state lives in embedded_comfy_client.py:_cleanup (lines ~149-169) and is called from Comfy.__aexit__ (line ~481).

# Design constraints / refinements over the user's notes
- Do NOT default to --highvram (breaks multi-model workflows that need offload). Default to Comfy's smart memory.
- /free is asynchronous — flush API must either drain via a no-op prompt or document the queue-flag semantics. Tests must account for this.
- Auto-flush should only trigger on model-set fingerprint change AND VRAM-headroom-below-threshold. Input/seed/prompt changes must NOT trigger flush.
- VIBECOMFY_WARM=auto|always|never controls policy (auto default).
- Session lifecycle: daemon-style for CLI (pidfile + URL file under out/sessions/<id>/) so multiple `vibecomfy run` invocations share one warm pool. Embedded session is just a Python context manager.
- Browser-only tasks: there are none in this plan (no UI work).
- No hard-halt prereq clauses: each milestone is independently shippable.

# Out of scope for this megaplan
- Multi-GPU session pooling.
- Distributed/remote session sharing across machines.
- Workflow-graph-level caching (Comfy already has --cache-lru / --cache-classic).
- Replacing the existing one-shot run/run_embedded APIs — they should remain as thin wrappers around the new session.
- Cloud/RunPod orchestration changes (the runpod worker can adopt EmbeddedSession in a separate plan).

# Suggested milestone shape (megaplan can refine during prep/plan)
M1: Session abstraction + embedded backend
  - vibecomfy/runtime/session.py: VibeSession protocol + EmbeddedSession holding a long-lived Comfy() across multiple .run(workflow) calls.
  - .flush() -> Comfy.clear_cache(); .reconfigure(config) -> Comfy.reconfigure().
  - Refactor run_embedded() to be a one-shot wrapper around EmbeddedSession.
  - Tests: two sequential runs in one session reuse models (assert via inspecting comfy.model_management.current_loaded_models after each run, or via a stub model manager); flush() drops them; reconfigure with same fingerprint is a no-op.

M2: Managed server backend
  - ServerSession: keeps comfy_server subprocess alive across .run() calls. Start = spawn + wait-ready. .flush() = POST /api/free with appropriate flags. .stop() = SIGTERM with timeout fallback to SIGKILL.
  - Daemon mode: `vibecomfy session start [--id NAME] [--vram-policy ...]` writes pidfile + URL to out/sessions/<id>/; `vibecomfy session stop <id>`; `vibecomfy session list`; `vibecomfy session flush <id>`.
  - `vibecomfy run` auto-discovers active session if one exists, else falls back to one-shot managed mode.
  - Tests: session start/stop, flush via HTTP, two runs share warmth, daemon survives across CLI invocations.

M3: Auto-flush policy + fingerprinting
  - vibecomfy/runtime/model_fingerprint.py: extract loader-node identity tuples from a workflow (CheckpointLoaderSimple.ckpt_name, LoraLoader.lora_name, UNETLoader.unet_name, VAELoader.vae_name, CLIPLoader.clip_name, DualCLIPLoader, etc. — discover the canonical set from the embedded fork's nodes).
  - Session.run() compares current workflow fingerprint to last-run fingerprint. If different AND free VRAM < threshold (configurable, default e.g. 2GB), call flush() before queueing.
  - VIBECOMFY_WARM env var: auto (default) | always | never.
  - Tests: same model + different seed -> no flush; different ckpt -> flush; auto-flush gated by VRAM headroom (mock get_free_memory).

M4: VRAM/cache flag plumbing + docs
  - Session config accepts: vram_policy ("auto"|"high"|"low"|"normal"), reserve_vram_gb, cache_policy ("smart"|"classic"|"lru:N"|"none"), disable_smart_memory.
  - Threaded through to embedded Configuration AND server CLI args.
  - docs/runtime_lifecycle.md: add "Warm sessions" section. docs/runtime_surface.md: document VibeSession API.
  - Smoke test: `vibecomfy session start && vibecomfy run x.json && vibecomfy run y.json && vibecomfy session stop` — second run is materially faster than first (timing-based, with tolerance).

# Open questions for the planning step
- Should `vibecomfy run` lazily start a session on first call and reuse it (implicit warm) vs always require `session start`? Lean toward explicit start for predictability.
- Does the runpod worker (reigh-workspace/reigh-worker) want to consume EmbeddedSession directly or talk to a local managed session? Probably embedded — it owns the process lifecycle already. Out of scope for this plan, but the API should not preclude it.
- Do we need partial flush (just CLIP, just VAE, just UNet)? Probably no — Comfy's pressure-driven eviction handles per-model decisions; only expose all-or-nothing flush at the session boundary.

# Success criteria (suggested — planning phase should refine)
- A `VibeSession` abstraction exists with both Embedded and Server backends.
- Two sequential `.run()` calls in one session share warm models (verified by test, not just timing).
- `flush()` is explicit and works for both backends. For the server backend, the test accounts for /free's queue-flag semantics.
- `vibecomfy session start/stop/list/flush` CLI commands exist and survive across separate `vibecomfy` invocations.
- Auto-flush policy fires on model-set change under VRAM pressure, never on seed/input changes.
- Existing `run` and `run_embedded` one-shot APIs continue to work unchanged (backwards compatibility).
- Documentation updated in docs/runtime_lifecycle.md and docs/runtime_surface.md.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Warm-runtime VibeSession (embedded + managed backends)

## Overview

Both runtime entry points are cold-per-call today: `run_embedded()` enters/exits a fresh `Comfy()` per generation (`vibecomfy/runtime/run.py:125-138`) whose `__aexit__` runs `unload_all_models()`, and `run()` spawns/terminates `comfyui serve` per call (`vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:27-50`). ComfyUI itself keeps models warm in-process and evicts under pressure — the work here is structural.

We add a `VibeSession` abstraction with two backends — `EmbeddedSession` (one long-lived `Comfy()`) and `ServerSession` (one long-lived `comfyui serve` subprocess) — plus a CLI session pool under `out/sessions/<id>/` (pidfile + URL file) so multiple `vibecomfy run` invocations share one warm pool. Existing `run`/`run_embedded` stay as one-shot wrappers (backwards-compatible).

Per the brief, the long-lived **managed** server is the default for CLI users. The plan changes `vibecomfy run`'s default runtime to `auto` and routes through a discovered session when one exists. The schema provider is taught to share the same URL so we don't accidentally spin up a second cold subprocess for `/object_info`. Default policy: keep-warm with smart memory; explicit `flush()` and a fingerprint-gated auto-flush handle model swaps under VRAM pressure.

Settled decisions carried forward (unchanged):
- `vibecomfy run --runtime auto` is the new default; auto-attaches to a warm session if any.
- Schema provider URL discovery before `get_schema_provider`; same URL into both call sites.
- Daemon folded into `vibecomfy/commands/session.py` (single file).
- Managed-mode warmth verified at the test layer (subprocess spawn count == 1 + `/api/free` payload assertions).
- ServerSession default port 8188 with `--port` override; auto-flush threshold default 2.0 GB.
- Pattern-based loader fingerprint: any `class_type` containing `"Loader"` contributes its non-edge string-valued slots.
- Shared runtime helpers move from `run.py` into `session.py` (one-direction import).
- `tests/test_cli.py:25-40` registry assertion gets `"session"` inserted between `"runtime"` and `"logs"`.
- `SessionConfig` has an `extra: dict[str, Any]` escape hatch; `from_workflow_metadata` partitions known keys into typed fields and unknown into `extra`; merge order: typed → extra → `VIBECOMFY_COMFY_CONFIGURATION` env override.

Resolutions added in this revision:
- **callers / correctness / FLAG-010 (helper signature compatibility)**: All three current callers of `_embedded_configuration` pass a `VibeWorkflow`, not a SessionConfig — verified at `vibecomfy/runtime/run.py:125`, `tests/test_runtime_run.py:116`, and `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py:56-60`. The plan therefore **keeps `_embedded_configuration(workflow: VibeWorkflow) -> Configuration` as the public signature** unchanged. Its body internally calls `SessionConfig.from_workflow_metadata(workflow)`, applies typed fields, then merges `extra`, then applies the `VIBECOMFY_COMFY_CONFIGURATION` env override last. The merge contract from v4 is preserved; only the **public signature** stays workflow-taking. A separate package-private helper `_embedded_configuration_for_session(config: SessionConfig) -> Configuration` is what `EmbeddedSession.start()` and `EmbeddedSession.reconfigure()` call directly. `_embedded_configuration(workflow)` is a thin one-liner wrapper around it. Existing tests (`test_embedded_configuration_uses_hiddenswitch_configuration_object` at `tests/test_runtime_run.py:98-121`) and the doctor probe (`commands/doctor.py:60`) keep working without modification.
- **all_locations / FLAG-011 (daemon constructor)**: Add `SessionConfig.from_dict(d: dict[str, Any]) -> SessionConfig` as a peer of `from_workflow_metadata`, sharing the same partition logic (apply known keys to typed fields, route the rest into `extra`). Step 6.1 daemon entrypoint calls `SessionConfig.from_dict(json.loads(args.config))`. Add a success criterion that requires `from_dict` to exist and to round-trip the same partition behavior as `from_workflow_metadata`.

Out of scope: multi-GPU pooling, distributed sessions, workflow-graph caching, replacing one-shot APIs, RunPod orchestration changes.

## Phase 1: Embedded session foundation

### Step 1: Add VibeSession protocol + EmbeddedSession + relocate shared helpers (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`, `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py`, `vibecomfy/runtime/run.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`. To avoid `run.py`↔`session.py` circular imports, this module owns the shared runtime helpers: move `RunResult` (`run.py:19-25`), `_prepare_prompt` (`run.py:28-42`), `_run_metadata` (`run.py:76-96`), `_collect_output_paths` (`run.py:204-215`), `_git_sha` (`run.py:99-109`), and the body of `_embedded_configuration` (`run.py:163-181`) into `session.py`. `run.py` imports from `session.py`; not the reverse.
2. **Update** `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py:1-11` so `from vibecomfy.runtime import RunResult` keeps working (re-export through `run.py`).
3. **Add** `SessionConfig` dataclass:
   ```python
   @dataclass
   class SessionConfig:
       vram_policy: Literal["auto","high","low","normal"] = "auto"
       reserve_vram_gb: float | None = None
       cache_policy: Literal["smart","classic","none"] | str = "smart"  # also "lru:N"
       disable_smart_memory: bool = False
       warm_policy: Literal["auto","always","never"] = "auto"
       auto_flush_vram_threshold_gb: float = 2.0
       port: int = 8188  # server-only
       extra: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)  # raw HiddenSwitch keys not yet promoted
   ```
   Plus a private `_partition_comfy_config(d: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[dict, dict]` that splits a raw HiddenSwitch config dict into typed-field kwargs vs `extra`. Mappings:
   - `reserve_vram` (number) → `reserve_vram_gb`
   - `highvram=True` / `lowvram=True` / `normalvram=True` → `vram_policy`
   - `cache_none=True` → `cache_policy="none"`; `cache_classic=True` → `cache_policy="classic"`; `cache_lru` (int) → `cache_policy="lru:N"`
   - `disable_smart_memory` → same name
   - All other keys → `extra` verbatim (e.g. `fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc`).
   Add two class methods that share the partition logic:
   ```python
   @classmethod
   def from_dict(cls, d: dict[str, Any]) -> "SessionConfig":
       typed_kwargs, extra = _partition_comfy_config(d or {})
       return cls(**typed_kwargs, extra=extra)

   @classmethod
   def from_workflow_metadata(cls, workflow) -> "SessionConfig":
       return cls.from_dict(workflow.metadata.get("comfy_configuration", {}))
   ```
   Document `extra` in the docstring as an intentional escape hatch; promote frequently-used keys to typed fields over time.
4. **Add** `class VibeSession(Protocol)`: `async start()`, `async run(workflow, *, backend="api") -> RunResult`, `async flush(*, unload_models=True, free_memory=True)`, `async reconfigure(config)`, `async stop()`, attribute `last_fingerprint`.
5. **Add** `class EmbeddedSession(VibeSession)`: holds one long-lived `Comfy()` across calls. `start()` calls `_embedded_configuration_for_session(self.config)` to build the Configuration, instantiates `Comfy(configuration=...)`, `__aenter__`s once. `run()` calls `comfy.queue_prompt_api(api_dict)`. `flush()` → `comfy.clear_cache()`. `reconfigure(SessionConfig)` → `comfy.reconfigure(_embedded_configuration_for_session(config))`. `stop()` calls `__aexit__(None, None, None)` and tolerates `model_mmap_residency` AttributeError.
6. **Define both helpers** in `session.py`, with the package-private one taking SessionConfig and the public one keeping its workflow-taking signature for backwards compatibility:
   ```python
   def _embedded_configuration_for_session(config: SessionConfig) -> Any | None:
       """Build a HiddenSwitch Configuration from a SessionConfig.

       Apply order: typed fields → config.extra → VIBECOMFY_COMFY_CONFIGURATION env override.
       Returns None when the resulting dict is empty (matches existing behavior at run.py:174-175).
       """
       merged: dict[str, Any] = {}
       # ... typed-field mapping (vram_policy → highvram/lowvram/normalvram, reserve_vram_gb → reserve_vram, cache_policy → cache_*, disable_smart_memory) ...
       merged.update(config.extra)
       env = os.environ.get("VIBECOMFY_COMFY_CONFIGURATION")
       if env:
           parsed = json.loads(env)
           if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
               raise ValueError("VIBECOMFY_COMFY_CONFIGURATION must be a JSON object")
           merged.update(parsed)
       if not merged:
           return None
       from comfy.client.embedded_comfy_client import default_configuration
       configuration = default_configuration()
       configuration.update(merged)
       return configuration

   def _embedded_configuration(workflow) -> Any | None:
       """Backwards-compatible wrapper. Existing callers pass a VibeWorkflow."""
       return _embedded_configuration_for_session(SessionConfig.from_workflow_metadata(workflow))
   ```
   Existing callers — `runtime/run.py:125`, `tests/test_runtime_run.py:116`, `commands/doctor.py:56-60` — are unchanged.
7. **Add pattern-based** `model_fingerprint(api_dict) -> tuple` in `session.py` (unchanged from v4): any `class_type` containing `"Loader"` (and not in `OVERRIDES_EXCLUDE`), plus members of `OVERRIDES_INCLUDE`, contributes its non-edge string-valued input slots as sorted `(class_type, slot, value)` triples.

### Step 2: Auto-flush gate inside `EmbeddedSession.run` (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Compute** `fp = model_fingerprint(api_dict)` before queueing. Resolve effective policy: `os.environ.get("VIBECOMFY_WARM")` overrides `config.warm_policy`. `never` → flush then queue; `always` → never auto-flush; `auto` → flush iff `last_fingerprint is not None and fp != last_fingerprint and _free_vram_gb() < threshold`.
2. After successful queue: `self.last_fingerprint = fp`.
3. **Add** `_free_vram_gb()` lazy-importing `comfy.model_management.get_free_memory()`; returns `float("inf")` on import/AttributeError.

### Step 3: Refactor `run_embedded` into a one-shot wrapper (`vibecomfy/runtime/run.py`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Rewrite** `run_embedded()` (`run.py:116-156`) as a thin wrapper around `EmbeddedSession.start()/run()/stop()`. Public signatures of `run_embedded` and `run_embedded_sync` unchanged.
2. **Delete** the relocated helper bodies from `run.py` and import them from `session.py`. Re-export `_embedded_configuration` from `run.py` so `vibecomfy.runtime.run._embedded_configuration` continues to resolve (the existing `tests/test_runtime_run.py:116` does `runtime_run_module._embedded_configuration(_workflow())` and must keep working).
3. **No change** required to `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` — its `from vibecomfy.runtime.run import _embedded_configuration` import (`commands/doctor.py:56`) continues to work via the re-export.

### Step 4: Tests for the embedded session (`tests/test_runtime_session.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `tests/test_runtime_session.py` reusing the `sys.modules` injection pattern from `tests/test_runtime_run.py:140-149`. `FakeComfy` records enter/exit counts plus `queue_prompt_api`/`clear_cache`/`reconfigure` calls.
2. **Cover (must)**:
   - Two `session.run(workflow_a)` calls produce **one** `__aenter__`/`__aexit__` pair.
   - `flush()` calls `clear_cache()` exactly once.
   - `reconfigure(SessionConfig(...))` passes a Configuration whose typed fields reflect SessionConfig.
   - **`SessionConfig.from_workflow_metadata` carry-through**: build a `VibeWorkflow` whose `metadata["comfy_configuration"] = {"reserve_vram": 12, "cache_none": True, "fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc": True}` (matching `ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame.py:347`). Assert: `config.reserve_vram_gb == 12`, `config.cache_policy == "none"`, `config.extra == {"fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc": True}`. Then call `_embedded_configuration_for_session(config)` and assert the resulting Configuration has `reserve_vram == 12`, `cache_none == True`, AND `fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc == True`.
   - **`SessionConfig.from_dict` mirror**: assert `SessionConfig.from_dict({"reserve_vram": 12, "cache_none": True, "fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc": True})` produces the same SessionConfig as the `from_workflow_metadata` test above (proves daemon path uses the same partition logic).
   - **`SessionConfig.from_dict` empty/None**: `from_dict({})` → all defaults, empty `extra`. `from_dict(None if accepted)` or absent metadata → same.
   - **Pattern-based fingerprint** (3 snapshot tests as in v4): `wan_t2v.api.json` (UNETLoader/CLIPLoader/VAELoader widget_0); `ltx2_3_t2v.api.json` (LowVRAMCheckpointLoader, LowVRAMAudioVAELoader, LTXAVTextEncoderLoader, LoraLoaderModelOnly); synthetic dict with `DualCLIPLoaderGGUF`/`UnetLoaderGGUF`. Edge references not included.
   - Auto-flush truth table with mocked `_free_vram_gb`.
   - `VIBECOMFY_WARM=never` / `=always` honored.
3. **Confirm** existing `tests/test_runtime_run.py:98-121` (`test_embedded_configuration_uses_hiddenswitch_configuration_object`) passes unchanged — it calls `runtime_run_module._embedded_configuration(_workflow())` which now routes through the re-exported wrapper.

## Phase 2: Managed server session + warm-default CLI

### Step 5: ServerSession backend (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`, `vibecomfy/runtime/client.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Extract** subprocess startup from `runtime/server.py:27-41` into `_spawn_comfy_server(config: SessionConfig) -> tuple[Process, str, log_handle]` in `session.py`. Translates SessionConfig to CLI flags: `--highvram|--lowvram|--normalvram` (skip when `auto`), `--reserve-vram <GB>`, `--disable-smart-memory`, `--cache-classic|--cache-lru N|--cache-none` (skip when `smart`), `--port <P>`. Reuse the 120-iteration `/system_stats` readiness loop.
2. **Add** `ServerSession(VibeSession)` to `session.py`. `start()` calls `_spawn_comfy_server(self.config)`. `run()` calls `ComfyClient(self.url).queue_prompt(api_dict)` with the auto-flush gate. `flush()` POSTs `/api/free` with `{"unload_models": True, "free_memory": True}`. `reconfigure(config)` is restart-on-change. `stop()` SIGTERM/15s/SIGKILL.
3. **Add** `ComfyClient.free(unload_models, free_memory)` in `runtime/client.py:30+`. One-line docstring: queue-async semantics.

### Step 6: Daemon CLI + warm-default routing + registry test update (`vibecomfy/commands/session.py`, `vibecomfy/commands/run.py`, `vibecomfy/commands/__init__.py`, `tests/test_cli.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `vibecomfy/commands/session.py` with argparse wiring for `start/stop/list/flush/status` AND the in-process daemon entrypoint. Single file. Invokable as `python -m vibecomfy.commands.session --daemon --id NAME --config <json>`. When run that way: `config_dict = json.loads(args.config)`; `session = ServerSession(SessionConfig.from_dict(config_dict))`; `await session.start()`; write `out/sessions/<id>/{pid,url,config.json}`; install SIGTERM handler that calls `await session.stop()` and removes the pidfile; sleep on `asyncio.Event`.
2. **Daemonize** from `session start` by `subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-m", "vibecomfy.commands.session", "--daemon", "--id", id_, "--config", json.dumps(config)], stdout=DEVNULL, stderr=open(log_path,"ab"), start_new_session=True)`. Parent waits up to 120 s for `out/sessions/<id>/url`.
3. **Subcommands**: `start [--id NAME=default] [--vram-policy auto|high|low|normal] [--reserve-vram-gb F] [--cache-policy smart|classic|lru:N|none] [--disable-smart-memory] [--warm-policy auto|always|never] [--port P=8188]`, `stop <id>`, `list`, `flush <id> [--unload-models/--no-unload-models] [--free-memory/--no-free-memory]`, `status <id>`.
4. **Add** `runtime/session.py::find_active_session(id="default") -> str | None`: read `out/sessions/<id>/url`; if pidfile present and `os.kill(pid, 0)` succeeds, return URL; else clean up and return `None`.
5. **Register** in `vibecomfy/commands/__init__.py:14-28` by adding `CommandSpec("session", "vibecomfy.commands.session")` between `"runtime"` and `"logs"`.
6. **Update** `tests/test_cli.py:25-40` to insert `"session"` between `"runtime"` and `"logs"` in the asserted ordered list.
7. **Make warm-managed the default for `vibecomfy run`** (`vibecomfy/commands/run.py`):
   - Change `--runtime` default from `"embedded"` to `"auto"`.
   - At the top of `_cmd_run`, **before** `get_schema_provider`: discover the session URL via `find_active_session("default")` (only when `args.runtime in ("auto","server")` and `--server-url` not set). Resolve effective runtime: `auto` + warm → server against that URL; `auto` + no warm → embedded one-shot; `server` + warm → reuse it; `server` + no warm → existing one-shot managed.
   - Pass the URL into `get_schema_provider("auto", server_url=session_url)` and into `run_sync(..., server_url=session_url, ...)`.

### Step 7: Tests for ServerSession + CLI (`tests/test_runtime_session.py`, `tests/test_session_cli.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **In** `tests/test_runtime_session.py` add `ServerSession` tests with monkeypatched `asyncio.create_subprocess_exec` and stub `httpx.AsyncClient`. Cover (must):
   - `start()` invokes subprocess with expected CLI argv given `SessionConfig(vram_policy="high", reserve_vram_gb=2.0, cache_policy="lru:3", disable_smart_memory=True, port=8200)`.
   - Two `session.run(...)` calls share one subprocess (spawn count == 1).
   - `flush()` POSTs `/api/free` with `{"unload_models": True, "free_memory": True}`.
   - `reconfigure(same_config)` is a no-op; `reconfigure(different_cli_args)` triggers stop+start.
   - `stop()` SIGTERMs once, falls back to SIGKILL after 15 s.
2. **Create** `tests/test_session_cli.py`. Mock `subprocess.Popen` so the "daemon" is replaced by a synchronous helper that writes `out/sessions/<id>/{pid,url,config.json}` with a synthetic alive pid. Assert: start returns 0 + creates files; list prints active id+URL; flush calls `ComfyClient.free(True, True)`; stop removes files; `find_active_session` returns URL when pid alive, `None` (and cleans up) when pid dead.
3. **Add daemon-config carry-through test** in `tests/test_session_cli.py`: simulate `python -m vibecomfy.commands.session --daemon --config '{"reserve_vram":12,"cache_none":true,"fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc":true,"port":8200}'` by directly calling the daemon entrypoint function with the parsed config dict; assert it instantiates `ServerSession(SessionConfig(reserve_vram_gb=12, cache_policy="none", port=8200, extra={"fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc": True}))` (mock `ServerSession.start` to record the constructor argument).
4. **Add** `tests/test_runtime_run.py` regression: `_cmd_run` with `--runtime auto` and a fake active session in `out/sessions/default/url` calls `run_sync` with that URL **and** constructs the schema provider with the same URL.

## Phase 3: Documentation + smoke

### Step 8: Docs (`docs/runtime_lifecycle.md`, `docs/runtime_surface.md`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Append** "Warm sessions" section to `docs/runtime_lifecycle.md`: Embedded vs Server, the `vibecomfy session` CLI, warm-policy rules, `/api/free` queue-flag semantics, `VIBECOMFY_WARM`, the pattern-based loader fingerprint, the `SessionConfig.extra` escape hatch, and the new `auto` default routing.
2. **Append** a `VibeSession` API block to `docs/runtime_surface.md` under "Embedded Surface".
3. **Update** `docs/runtime_lifecycle.md:3` to reflect `vibecomfy run` now defaults to `auto`.

### Step 9: Smoke (`scripts/warm_session_smoke.py`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Add** a manual smoke script using the embedded session — runs two cheap workflows back-to-back through one `EmbeddedSession`, prints elapsed deltas.

## Execution Order

1. Steps 1–4: embedded session + helper relocation + extra-config carry-through + tests first.
2. Steps 5–7: ServerSession + daemon CLI (using `SessionConfig.from_dict`) + warm-default routing + `test_cli.py` registry update.
3. Step 8: docs.
4. Step 9: smoke script.

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_cli.py` — registry assertion still passes after adding `"session"`.
2. `pytest tests/test_runtime_run.py` — `run_embedded` refactor + helper relocation preserves existing behavior; `test_embedded_configuration_uses_hiddenswitch_configuration_object` continues to pass via the workflow-taking re-exported wrapper.
3. `pytest tests/test_runtime_session.py` — embedded + server session unit tests, including `from_workflow_metadata`/`from_dict` partition tests and pattern-based fingerprint snapshot tests against `wan_t2v.api.json` and `ltx2_3_t2v.api.json`.
4. `pytest tests/test_session_cli.py` — daemon CLI flow + daemon-config carry-through test.
5. Full `pytest` — confirm no collateral damage. In particular, `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py:56-60` still imports `_embedded_configuration` from `runtime.run` and passes a probe `VibeWorkflow`; this must keep working.
6. Manual on RunPod (info-only): `python scripts/warm_session_smoke.py`.

    "Tests use the existing `sys.modules` injection pattern from `tests/test_runtime_run.py:140-149`. No new test deps; pyproject.toml stays at httpx + pytest + pytest-asyncio.",
    "Public `_embedded_configuration(workflow)` signature is preserved unchanged. The new SessionConfig-taking helper `_embedded_configuration_for_session(config)` lives alongside it; both share the same merge logic (typed \u2192 extra \u2192 env-override). EmbeddedSession internals call the SessionConfig-taking variant; existing call sites at runtime/run.py:125, tests/test_runtime_run.py:116, and commands/doctor.py:56-60 keep using the workflow-taking wrapper.",
    "SessionConfig has both `from_workflow_metadata(workflow)` and `from_dict(d)` constructors, sharing a `_partition_comfy_config(d)` helper that maps known keys to typed fields and routes the rest to `extra`.",
    "Pattern-based loader detection: any class_type containing 'Loader' contributes its non-edge string-valued slots. Empty initial OVERRIDES_INCLUDE/OVERRIDES_EXCLUDE.",
    "Helper ownership: shared runtime helpers move into `session.py`; `run.py` imports from session.py and re-exports `_embedded_configuration` for backwards compatibility. `runtime/__init__.py` re-exports `RunResult`.",
    "tests/test_cli.py:25-40 ordered COMMANDS list is updated to include 'session' between 'runtime' and 'logs'.",
    "`vibecomfy run --runtime auto` is the new default. Auto-attaches to a warm session if one exists; falls back to embedded one-shot when no session is active.",
    "Managed warmth verified at the test layer via subprocess-spawn-count assertions and `/api/free` payload assertions; no /history or websocket polling in v1.",
    "Daemon mode uses `subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-m', 'vibecomfy.commands.session', '--daemon', '--id', id_, '--config', json.dumps(config_dict)], start_new_session=True)`. The daemon parses --config JSON and calls SessionConfig.from_dict(...).",
    "Pidfile + URL file land under `out/sessions/<id>/`. Default session id = 'default'. Stale pidfile auto-cleanup when the recorded pid is dead.",
    "Auto-flush threshold default = 2.0 GB free VRAM. Fixed port 8188 default for ServerSession with `--port` override.",
    "`run_embedded` and `run`/`run_sync` retain identical public signatures."
  ],
  "delta_from_previous_percent": 54.3,
  "structure_warnings": []
}

  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-007",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-008",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-143",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",

    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-142",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting-infrastructure review still finds one missing check. in `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md`, sprint 2's verification matrix and risk register both single out `self_refiner` as a distinct drift-upgrade surface with its own smoke and its own silent-behavior-change risk, but the revised v4 body updates the file without keeping that supporting verification step in the actual execution checklist.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
